# EP08 scene library v2 (11/18)

Every clip in `<EP08>\1_work\clips` (174 clips: 81 from v1 and 93 new, all 720p+ SDR) was checked by eye, one frame every 2 s, with brightened full-size grabs for anything small or dark. Clip titles and the old beats_v1.md notes are NOT trusted: several titles are wrong, and those are corrected below. Times are seconds into the clip file (m:ss).
Tags: [PROOF n] = story_review_v2.md section 4 numbering, [SUICIDE] never usable, [GORE] cut away, [DARK] needs brightening, [TEXT] on-screen text.

## Must-have moments with NO true shot in the library (reword the line or fall back)
- The Conjuring: birds hitting the windows (only birds swooping at the house at night, co_mother_c 1:37-1:50); Carolyn's scissors over Christine (over April exists: c1_exorcism_b 0:44); an all-clocks-at-3:07 montage (single clocks only).
- The Conjuring 2: Janet thrown into the wall (nearest: Janet flung back into her room, c2_cz_voices 7:52-7:54, dark); Lorraine's visions being blocked; Janet levitating/teleported into the chair.
- Annabelle Comes Home: a clear skeletal Ferryman reaching for Mary Ellen (coin-eyed souls and Mary Ellen dragged by an unseen force exist); the hellhound hunting Bob in the YARD (on screen it attacks the car with Judy inside, and Bob smashes his guitar on it on the car, ach_wolf 0:57-1:00); Annabelle Higgins screaming in Judy's face.
- Annabelle (2014): books thrown at the baby; the claw tearing Mia's arm (only the scratches afterwards).
- Annabelle: Creation: Janice THROWN off the stairlift (the ride up in the dark exists, cr_rt_chair 0:36-0:58; it cuts before the throw); the orphans arriving; Janice escaping through the closet wall.
- The Devil Made Me Do It: Arne walking BLOODY on the road (the walk exists without blood); the ward windows bursting.
- Last Rites: a separate "grinning man" third ghost.
- The Nun II: the goat demon clearly on screen; "deaths across Europe"; Maurice's body bending and snapping.
- The Nun: the inverted cross on Frenchie's neck / Valak escaping into Frenchie.

## Must-have moments FOUND (key ones)
- The Conjuring, Sadie FOUND DEAD in the yard: c1_dog_b 0:53.5-0:56.0 (morning, wide; Carolyn holds April, Roger runs in, the dog's body at the right edge). Recap clip: red logo top-right and a narrator over the audio (mute the clip audio). NOT the dog: the pigeon by the wall c1_dog_b 1:38-1:39.5; Sadie alive c1_dog_b 0:24-0:29 and 0:51-0:53.
- The Nun II delivery kid lifted by the throat: n2_deliv 2:17.2-2:19.7 (last safe frame 2:19.7, hard cut at 2:20.0). The kid reads as a girl in close-ups (0:36-0:48) and as a boy at a glance.
- ACH lock: all three girls push the case shut at ach_end 1:17-1:20, and the key is turned at 1:20-1:22 by a hand in Daniela's sleeve.

## Age-gated (not downloaded; owner cookie commands in qa/age_gated_v2.txt)
c1_s07_witch, c1_s10_condemn, a14_s07_church, dm_s01_shower, dm_s02_child, dm_s03_occultist, dm_s04_hurt, n1_s03_child, n1_s04_mirror, n1_s08_lantern, n1_cz_first10 (plus the v1 ones in qa/age_gated.txt). Dropped: dm_morgue_sf (480p).

## The Conjuring (2013)

Method: every contact sheet read; key moments re-checked on full-size or 0.25-0.5 s frames (frames in SP\zoom\c1\). Audio was transcribed with faster-whisper to check for voice-over (tx_*.txt in SP\zoom\c1\). Only c1_dog_b has a narrator. Times are clip seconds as m:ss. "Letterboxed" means 2.39:1 picture inside the frame, with black bars top and bottom.

### Proof moments (The Conjuring)
1. Every clock stopped at 3:07:
   - c1_dog_b@1:23.5-1:27.5 (grandfather clock close-up, about 3:07; recap VO, Horrors bug top-right)
   - c1_dog_b@0:58-1:01 (same clock, Carolyn passing)
   - c1_land@2:20-2:26 (a desk alarm clock at about 3:07; clean, but burned-in subtitles; this is the Warrens' research scene, not a Perron clock)
   - The ar_perron and feed mantel clocks are not readable. No "every clock" montage was found.
2. Cellar match plus clapping hands:
   - c1_cz_clap@4:30-4:53.6 (hands at 4:53.0)
   - c1_cz_hideclip@4:52-5:15 (hands at 5:14.5)
   - c1_best / c1_clap@2:16-2:40 (hands at about 2:39.5; c1_clap carries the Movieclips bug)
3. Bathsheba on top of the wardrobe dropping onto Andrea:
   - c1_bedroom@1:50.5-1:52 (crouch at 110.5-111.5 s, then lunge; very dark, needs brightening; 720p)
   - c1_cz_hideclip@7:16 (brief)
   - Andrea sliding on the rug after: c1_bedroom@2:14-2:16
4. Nancy dragged by her hair:
   - c1_feed@1:24-1:41 (dragged 1:27-1:37; hair yanked up 1:40-1:41)
   - co_mother_b@5:50-6:10 (clean ClipZone 4K)
   - Christine's foot pulled: c1_sleep@0:02.0-0:03.0 (wide; leg jerks toward the foot of the bed; brief and dim). It is not readable in c1_dog_b.
5. Birds hammering the house:
   - co_mother_c@1:37-1:50. These are black birds swooping at the house, porch and cars at night during the climax. They are not clearly hitting the windows; the "birds hammering windows" shot itself is MISSING.
   - c1_dog_b@1:38-1:39.5 shows a pigeon on the ground by the foundation wall (morning). That is NOT the dog.
6. Carolyn under the sheet spitting blood; the chair levitating:
   - co_mother_c@3:00-3:34 (blood soaking the sheet) and co_mother_c@0:00-0:47 (clean sheet)
   - Levitation: co_mother_c@4:00-4:24 (flip at 4:14)
   - c1_dog_b@7:12-7:20 (VO)
7. Carolyn with scissors over April:
   - c1_exorcism_b@0:44.2-0:44.8 (scissors raised over April's head, wide)
   - c1_exorcism_b@0:48-0:49.5 (close-up; bloody mouth)
   - Scissors over Christine: MISSING. The Christine attack seems to be off-screen in these clips.
8. Dead dog: FOUND. c1_dog_b@0:53.5-0:56.0, morning, wide. Carolyn holds April in a pink robe by the tree, Roger runs in, and Sadie lies dead on the ground at frame right.
   - Caveats: this is the only source. It carries the Horrors Worldwide bug top-right and the recap narrator's VO ("found dead outside the house by April the next morning").
   - Do not confuse it with Sadie ALIVE on the porch (c1_dog_b@0:24-0:29, c1_cz_annstory@7:22-7:38), Sadie alive in the yard at night (c1_dog_b@0:51-0:53), or the pigeon by the wall (c1_dog_b@1:38-1:39.5).

Other key questions:
- Bruises:
  - c1_cz_hideclip@0:13-0:16 (arm and shoulder, clean 4K)
  - c1_dog_b@0:56-0:58 (legs)
  - c1_dog_b@1:34.5-1:36 (shoulder, Roger looks)
- The exorcism:
  - co_mother_c@0:00-4:46 (cellar rite)
  - c1_exorcism_b@1:32-1:58 (Ed's hand on Carolyn's forehead, crawl-space finale)
- Lorraine's happy beach memory: c1_exorcism_b@1:20-1:21, @1:27-1:28 and @1:42-1:43 (family on the beach). Also c1_dog_b@7:48 (VO).
- Bathsheba's past killings (backstory): not shown, only told. c1_land@0:48-1:22 is dialogue with subtitles.
- NEVER USE:
  - c1_dog_b@3:27.5-3:29: the hanging corpse behind Ed [SUICIDE]
  - nooses in the crawl space: co_mother@0:52-0:58, co_mother_b@2:22-2:28, c1_dog_b@5:42-5:44 [SUICIDE-adjacent]
- Brad's bitten cheek: not identified in any clip (unclear).

## The Conjuring 2 (2016)

Notes: all timecodes are clip time (m:ss). Proof numbering = story_review_v2 section 4, block "The Conjuring 2 (#6)": 1 painting + Valak gliding at Lorraine, 2 Janet thrown into wall, 3 possessed Janet in Bill's chair / old man's voice, 4 Crooked Man + zoetrope + Billy, 5 Ed fighting up the stairs in the storm, 6 Lorraine shouts Valak's name, Valak pulled into the dark, 7 (avoid) Janet at window / Ed impaling vision. Tags: [SUICIDE] = Janet-at-window material, [GORE] = Ed impale vision / bodies, [DARK] = needs brightening, [TEXT] = on-screen text in the film.
Characters: Ed, Lorraine, Judy (Warrens' daughter), Peggy Hodgson (mother), Janet (red pyjamas / red jumper), Margaret (older sister), Billy (little brother), Johnny, Bill Wilkins (old man ghost), Valak (demon nun), Crooked Man, Maurice Grosse (glasses, grey suit), Anita Gregory (sceptic, dark hair), Vic Nottingham (neighbour, moustache), Peggy's friend/neighbour Peggy Nottingham.

### Proof moments (The Conjuring 2)
- PROOF 1, Valak painting in the Warrens' house, then Valak gliding at Lorraine: c2_painting@2:56-3:29 (painting on the wall 2:56-3:13, hands out of the frame 3:16, glide at Lorraine 3:26-3:29). Backup c2_s01_nun@1:20-1:51 (lower quality, Movieclips bug). Valak looming in the study dark: c2_painting@0:58-1:11 and @1:52-2:17.
- PROOF 2, Janet hurled across the bedroom into the wall: MISSING. No clip shows Janet thrown into a wall. Nearest substitutes: c2_cz_voices@7:52-7:54 (Janet yanked off the floor and flung back into her room, dark); c2_ar_someone@1:37-1:40 (same moment, dark); c2_cz_chair@6:50-7:04 (Janet's bed bucking under her, dark); c2_cz_crooked@7:38-7:39 (fireguard flies across the room, then Janet collapses at 7:46-7:49).
- PROOF 3, possessed Janet in Bill's chair with the old man's voice: chair visuals in c2_cz_billy@4:02-4:07 and @7:30-7:47; c2_bill@0:00-0:05 and @3:27-3:30; c2_cz_voices@0:00-0:05 and @3:28-3:45. In the long take between, Janet is out of focus and dark. Old man's voice with a visible face: the sofa interview, c2_grave@0:38-0:43 / @1:16-1:23 / @1:58-2:16; c2_cz_billy@1:44-1:51 / @3:06-3:23. Janet possessed with white eyes: c2_cz_finale@6:04-6:07.
- PROOF 4, the Crooked Man, zoetrope and Billy: zoetrope spinning in the tent, c2_cz_crooked@4:54-5:09. Dog turns into the Crooked Man beside Billy: c2_cz_crooked@6:39-6:41 or c2_crooked@0:58-1:03. Crooked Man lunges: c2_cz_crooked@6:50-6:51 or c2_crooked@1:15-1:16. The Crooked Man does not unfold out of the zoetrope toward Billy in any clip. The zoetrope and Billy scenes are separate, and only Ed is with the zoetrope (c2_cz_finale@4:54-5:09).
- PROOF 5, Ed fighting up the stairs in the storm: c2_cz_finale@0:00-1:17 (wind and debris throw Ed down, he forces through), @2:30-2:37 (stairs, dark), @4:20-4:53 (climbing the dark landing), @5:50-5:53 (wading through flood water). Most of it is [DARK]. Backup c2_s09_name@1:28-2:01.
- PROOF 6, Lorraine shouts Valak's name and Valak is pulled into Hell: c2_cz_finale@7:14-7:51 (pinned 7:14-7:38, Valak dragged back 7:39-7:41, Lorraine screams the name 7:46, Valak tears apart and vanishes 7:48-7:51). Same scene, short and clean: c2_name@0:32-0:60. Backup c2_s10_defiler@1:24-1:55. Bible with VALAK scratched in (setup): c2_cz_finale@3:42-3:57, c2_name_b@0:56-1:07 [TEXT].
- PROOF 7 / CAUTION:
  - [SUICIDE] Janet at the window and on the curtain: c2_cz_finale@6:08-7:06 and @8:00-8:11; c2_name@0:12-0:16 and @0:24-0:29; c2_s10_defiler@0:12-0:57, @1:20-1:23 and @2:04-2:15.
  - [GORE] Ed's impaling vision: c2_cz_vision@6:24-6:29; flashes in c2_ar_premonition@0:25-0:26 and @0:36-0:37.
  - The real tree-stump spike, which the review allows ("use only the stump"): c2_cz_finale@6:29-6:36, c2_name@0:15-0:16, c2_s10_defiler@0:40-0:42. It is in the window-rescue sequence, so cut tight.
  - Amityville bodies [GORE]: c2_cz_vision@2:54-3:12.
- Key question, Bill Wilkins as a ghost: c2_vision@0:00-0:55 (clear, Bill in his chair talking with Lorraine). Also c2_cz_chair@1:42-1:45 (Bill on the TV screen in his chair); c2_ar_someone@0:40-1:01 and c2_cz_voices@6:54-7:17 (Bill in his chair rising, dark); jump scares at c2_ar_someone@2:38-2:40 and c2_cz_voices@9:22-9:23.
- Key question, Valak standing behind Bill (hiding behind him): c2_vision@0:56-0:57, Valak close @0:57-0:58, Lorraine's nosebleed @0:58. Best shot for "hides behind the ghost of an old man".
- Key question, Lorraine's blocked visions: MISSING as an explicit "she can't see" beat. Closest: c2_vision@0:56-0:59 (Valak revealed, Lorraine snapped out with a nosebleed). Lorraine attacked inside her Amityville vision: c2_cz_vision@6:12-6:23 (Valak in the mirror behind her, Valak close, Lorraine choked). Lorraine's remembered flashbacks of Valak: c2_cz_finale@3:04-3:13.
- Key question, Janet levitating / teleported in the chair: MISSING (no shot of Janet floating, or waking in Bill's chair). Nearest: Janet woken out of her tied-down bed onto the living-room floor and dragged by the string, c2_cz_voices@6:24-7:54 and c2_ar_someone@0:12-1:40 (dark). Possible float before the fall at c2_crooked@2:09-2:11 / c2_cz_crooked@7:46-7:49, unclear.
- Other usable moments: Valak in the Amityville mirror behind Lorraine, c2_cz_vision@6:12-6:18. Janet's possessed grin hidden in the cupboard, c2_janet@3:40-3:41. Knives standing on the kitchen table, c2_janet@1:36-1:45. Crucifixes turning upside down, c2_cz_voices@8:14-8:20 and c2_ar_someone@1:54-2:07 (dark). Bill on the TV in his chair, c2_cz_chair@1:42-1:45.

## Annabelle Comes Home (2019)

Frame-scanned: all contact sheets of all 17 clips, plus about 230 brightened full-size frames in scratchpad\zoom\ach\ (montages are `<key>_m_<t>.jpg`). Several keys are duplicates or overlaps of the same scene. Offsets: ach_bride_judy = ach_bride +4 s · ach_ferry = ach_cz_coineyes -110 s · ach_hands = ach_cz_coineyes -297 s · co_doll = ach_hands -2 s · ach_ferry_b = ach_devil_cz +28 s · ach_end = ach_devil_cz -224 s · ach_s03_youdid = ach_room -112 s · ach_tv = ach_room -278 s · ach_wb_basement / ach_wb_tv = ach_room (same timing) · ach_wolf = ach_cz_stabs -212 s.
Character key: Judy = little girl with a dark bob, nightgown or sweater with tulips · Mary Ellen = blonde babysitter in a pink sweater · Daniela = dark-haired friend in a rainbow-striped sweater (ribbed cuffs in pink and light blue) · Bob = boy in a denim jacket · Ed/Lorraine Warren.
Several metadata titles are wrong (see the TITLE WRONG notes).

### Proof moments (Annabelle Comes Home)
- PROOF 1 (spirit shoves Ed into the street, truck headlights): ach_case@2:05-2:09 (Ed on hands and knees in the road, truck headlights bearing down). Ghosts at the cemetery gate at 1:44-1:52 and 1:56-2:00. The actual SHOVE is not clearly visible (2:02-2:04 is a blur) [DARK].
- PROOF 2 (lamp silhouettes: doll rising, then Bee, then horned demon): ach_hands@1:04-1:15 (doll 1:04, rising doll shape 1:05-1:07, pigtailed girl 1:09-1:10, knife figure 1:11-1:14, horned demon 1:15). Demon face beside Judy at 1:17-1:18. Also co_doll@1:02-1:16 and ach_cz_coineyes@6:01-6:15.
- PROOF 3 (Judy under the sheets, doll beside her, Annabelle Higgins screams in her face): doll beside Judy at ach_hands@0:32-0:34 and 0:36-0:37 (also co_doll@0:30-0:35). The "Annabelle Higgins screams into her face" part is MISSING/unclear: at 0:38-0:39 only a motion-blurred screaming face that looks like Judy herself.
- PROOF 4 (skeletal Ferryman with coins, reaching for Mary Ellen in the hallway, flashlight beam): only PARTIAL. ach_ferry@1:58-2:01 / ach_cz_coineyes@3:48-3:51 show coin-eyed souls with a small, dark skull-like face by a lantern. Mary Ellen is dragged by an unseen force at ach_ferry@1:46-1:56 and 2:01-2:06, and runs the hallway with the flashlight at ach_ferry@2:06-2:14. A clear skeletal Ferryman reaching for her in the hallway is MISSING. Coins: ach_cz_coineyes@1:04-1:10, ach_ferry@1:38-1:44.
- PROOF 5 (Black Shuck in the yard, Bob swings his guitar into its face): ach_wolf@0:59-1:00 (Bob smashes the guitar over the hound on the car; the hound is only a dark mass) [DARK]. Claws tear the car roof at ach_wolf@0:43-0:50. Bob holds the smashed guitar at ach_devil_cz@5:30-5:40.
- PROOF 6 (possessed Daniela in the Bride's veil lunging at Mary Ellen, projector light): ach_devil_cz@2:26-2:40 (veiled Daniela faces Judy, then lunges at Mary Ellen) and @3:06-3:12 (veiled Daniela lit by the projector, Ed's film behind her). Burning-veil exorcism film at @3:38-3:40.
- PROOF 7 (artifact room, case door won't close as the girls push): ach_end@0:45-0:58 and 1:02-1:20 (Mary Ellen and Judy push the glass, Annabelle inside, Daniela joins at 1:17). Same at ach_devil_cz@4:29-5:04.
- Ferryman chasing Mary Ellen: see PROOF 4. Best overall: ach_ferry@1:46-2:14. The Ferryman's tomb vision (coin-eyed corpses, Mary Ellen with coins on her eyes holding Annabelle): ach_devil_cz@1:04-1:56 or ach_ferry_b@1:36-2:26 [DARK].
- Bride possessing Daniela: ach_cz_stabs@3:24 (ring on her finger) and @3:29-3:32 (the Bride spews blood into Daniela's mouth) [GORE-light]. The result is at ach_devil_cz@2:26-2:32. The Bride alone (appearing to Judy in daylight): ach_bride_judy@2:25-2:27 or ach_bride@2:22-2:23.
- Black Shuck / hellhound hunting Bob in the yard: on screen it attacks JUDY in the car (ach_wolf@0:43-0:50). A shot of it hunting Bob alone is MISSING. Bob fighting it: ach_wolf@0:57-1:00.
- Doll in Judy's bed / Annabelle beside Judy under the sheets: ach_hands@0:32-0:37 (best, no logo). Doll close-ups: ach_hands@0:48-0:53 (through the lamp lens) and ach_case@1:01-1:03 (back seat).
- Demon trying to take Judy's soul: ach_end@0:14-0:17 and 0:36-0:38 (horned demon draws white soul-mist from Judy's mouth). Also ach_devil_cz@3:58-4:00.
- Cursed objects waking up: typewriter "miss me?" ach_cz_stabs@1:40-1:43 [TEXT]. Ghost hand at the piano ach_room@3:32-3:43. Haunted TV ach_room@4:58-5:22. Toy monkey ach_room@5:56. Samurai armor ach_room@0:12-0:22 and ach_devil_cz@4:10. Lamp turning on ach_hands@0:14-0:17. Feeley Meeley ach_devil_cz@3:16-3:22. Bells shelf ach_cz_coineyes@7:20-7:25.
- Nobody dying: no on-screen death in any clip. Ed survives the truck (ach_case@2:18-2:37, Ed and Lorraine standing). All three girls stand unharmed after the lock (ach_end@1:22-1:31). Bob is alive at ach_devil_cz@5:30-5:40.
- Three girls force the doll back into the case and lock it (who holds the key?): closed by all three at ach_end@1:17-1:20. The key turns at ach_end@1:20-1:22 (= ach_devil_cz@5:04-5:05). The hand on the key has a pink-and-light-blue ribbed cuff that matches DANIELA's sweater (her cuffs are visible at ach_room@1:31-1:40), and Daniela's striped arm reaches to the lock at 1:19-1:20. So it is most likely Daniela, which agrees with Flag 3, but no face is in the shot. Three-girl wide after the lock: ach_end@1:22-1:31.
- Feeley Meeley game: ach_devil_cz@3:16-3:22 (game box on the carpet, hands from the board grabbing) [DARK]. Not in ach_hands (that title is wrong).
- Haunted TV: ach_room@4:58-5:50 (also ach_tv@0:22-1:12, ach_wb_tv, ach_wb_basement, ach_cz_stabs@0:00-0:24).

## Annabelle (2014)

Notes for the editor:
- Several keys are the same footage at different offsets. a14_wb_baby = a14_cz_haunted (same timecodes). a14_basement = a14_cz_haunted minus 174 s. a14_s03_rumble = a14_cz_attic minus 104 s. a14_stairs = a14_cz_attic minus 241 s. a14_cz_sleeping = a14_cz_invasion minus 102 s. a14_baby = a14_cz_soul minus 170 s.
- Titles are wrong in places. a14_basement ("basement storage chase") contains NO basement: it is the living-room/nursery hide-and-seek with Leah and the life-size Annabelle figure at the window. The real basement storage, elevator and stairwell footage is in a14_cz_attic, a14_s03_rumble and a14_stairs.
- ClipZone clips (a14_cz_*) have no watermark during the film, only an end card. The cz_attic, cz_haunted and cz_soul clips are full frame. cz_invasion and cz_sleeping are letterboxed.
- Never seen in any clip: books being thrown at Leah, and a demon claw actually tearing Mia's arm. Only the scratches afterwards are on screen (see the summary).
- "Mia's arm" scratches are thin red claw marks on her right forearm. They are not gory.

### Proof moments (Annabelle 2014)
1. Kitchen/stove fire: the popcorn flames up at a14_fire@1:26-1:29. The engulfed kitchen with Mia in the doorway is at a14_fire@1:48-1:51. Mia on the floor in smoke is at a14_fire@1:58-2:20. The Conjuring Archives bug is in both letterbox bars.
2. Empty carriage and the truck: a14_cz_haunted@1:28-1:51 (impact 1:41.5-1:43, crushed carriage 1:50-1:51). a14_wb_baby has the same timecodes. Alternatives are a14_s05_carriage@1:20-1:41 (Movieclips logo).
3. Basement storage and elevator: storage corridor a14_cz_attic@0:54-1:15. Dark figure behind Mia at a14_cz_attic@2:52-2:57 [DARK]. Elevator doors opening back onto the basement at a14_cz_attic@3:20-3:23 and 3:34-3:42. Alternative a14_s03_rumble@1:42-2:07.
4. Claws on Mia's arm: the claw actually tearing her arm is MISSING (never on screen). The resulting scratches are at a14_cz_attic@3:12-3:15 (best, clean full frame), with alternatives a14_cz_attic@5:20-5:21, a14_s03_rumble@1:28-1:33 and a14_stairs@1:18-1:19. The horned demon itself is at a14_cz_attic@5:06.0-5:06.4 (best) and a14_stairs@1:04.9-1:05.25. This is in the stairwell, not the apartment.
5. Father Perez hurled through the air at the church door: a14_wb_want@5:26-5:27 (wide, he flies off the steps), with the set-up at 5:20-5:25. The aftermath (doll beside the blood pool and his hand) is at 5:32-5:36 [GORE mild].
6. Empty crib, doll in Leah's place: doll in the crib at a14_baby@0:46-0:47 (= a14_cz_soul@3:36-3:37). Empty crib at a14_baby@1:14-1:19 and a14_cz_soul@1:08-1:09.
- Books thrown at the baby/crib: MISSING. In a14_basement@0:00-0:07 and a14_cz_haunted@2:54-3:11 Mia only picks up books next to Leah. Nothing is thrown.
- Demon stealing baby Leah: a14_baby@1:20-1:23 (= a14_cz_soul@4:08-4:11). Leah in a white sleeper slides out of the nursery doorway into the dark. Put it with the empty crib at a14_baby@1:14-1:19 and the doll in the crib at 0:46.
- The trade, baby for soul: "your soul" written in red on the nursery window at a14_baby@2:18-2:23 (= a14_cz_soul@5:06-5:11). The "her soul" scrawl is on the nursery ceiling at a14_baby@0:28-0:39 and 0:58. Both are in-scene text [TEXT].
- Evelyn's decision only: a14_cz_soul@8:30-8:35 (Evelyn standing in the open window holding the doll). Cut before 8:36.0. Her fall starts at 8:36.3, and the empty window plus John rushing over at 8:37-8:43 is also [SUICIDE]. The build-up is a14_cz_soul@7:58-8:13 (Evelyn kneeling by John and Mia). Caution: a14_cz_soul@6:54-7:57 is Mia herself on the window ledge with the doll (suicide-attempt context).
- Annabelle Higgins' throat: NEVER USE. It is at a14_cz_invasion@7:51-8:23 and a14_cz_sleeping@6:07-6:41 [SUICIDE] [GORE]. Safe Higgins shots are a14_cz_invasion@5:36-5:39 (holding the doll, no blood) and her ghost behind Perez at a14_wb_want@5:20-5:21.
- universe: contains no Annabelle (2014) material (only The Conjuring, The Conjuring 2 and The Devil Made Me Do It).

## Annabelle: Creation (2017)

Names used: Janice = blonde curly-haired orphan with a leg brace (teal nightgown at night, brown dress by day). Linda = blonde bob orphan. Carol / Nancy = the two older dark-haired orphans (the one in the barn wears a green blouse; which name belongs to which actress is not readable from the frames). Kate = Black orphan girl. Tierney = small girl with a brown bob. Charlotte = Sister Charlotte (nun habit by day, white nightgown at night). Samuel = the bearded dollmaker. Esther = his wife: auburn hair, a scar over the left side of her face in the present-day scenes. Bee = the little dark-haired girl in pigtails (flashbacks, and the demon's disguise).

Warning: several titles are wrong. cr_stair is NOT the stairlift; it is the barn and possession scene. No clip shows the stairlift throw itself.

### Proof moments (Annabelle: Creation)
- PROOF 1, stairlift reversing and Janice thrown down the stairwell: MISSING. Closest is the build-up in cr_soul@1:12-2:41: Janice on the stairlift in the dark, then an overhead of the stairwell at 2:38-2:41; the throw is cut off. cr_stair does NOT contain it (the title is wrong).
- PROOF 2, the "Sister Charlotte" figure wheeling Janice into the barn: cr_ar_barn@0:22-0:40 has the best full sequence, including the hooded silhouette against the sun at 0:30-0:32, but it has the logo bars. For clean full frame use cr_stair@0:24-0:39 or cr_wheel@1:20-1:33 (silhouette at 1:22).
- PROOF 3, Bee demonic plus black bile into Janice's mouth: cr_stair@2:00-2:08 (bile 2:05-2:08) is the cleanest; also cr_wheel@2:52-2:59 and cr_ar_barn@1:58-2:06.5. Bee's face is hazy and dark in all of them; a clearly "demonic" face is unclear or not seen.
- PROOF 4, Samuel's fingers bending back around the crucifix: cr_sam@0:16-0:28. The first bend is at about 0:18.5; cut on it, at about 0:18.5-0:19.3. Alt: cr_wheel@8:44-8:56. [GORE-medium]
- PROOF 5, Linda at the well, hand pulls her in, Charlotte saves her: cr_well@1:30-1:58 (grab 1:36-1:37, pulled 1:38-1:41, saved 1:44-1:52). Walk to the well: cr_well@0:00-0:46.
- PROOF 6, possessed Janice walking upright, then lunging at Linda: walking in cr_trapped@4:12-4:14 and 4:24-4:26, and cr_cz_orphan@2:36-3:02; lunge and knife in cr_trapped@5:50-6:02.
- PROOF 7, Esther's body on the wall: GORE, NEVER USE. It appears at cr_cz_orphan@2:14-2:27 and cr_esther_scenes@4:18-4:32. Avoid both ranges.
- Bee and the grieving Samuel and Esther: Bee at the tea table in cr_esther_scenes@2:16-2:32 and cr_wasnt@0:00-0:03. Esther and Samuel's quiet dinner (flashback, note found) in cr_esther_scenes@0:36-1:12. Samuel and a priest blessing Bee's room in cr_cz_orphan@0:00-0:34. Samuel grieving on the porch with Linda in cr_wheel@5:00-7:00. Bible pages lining the closet in cr_wasnt@1:04-1:11. A clear grieving scene for both of them together: MISSING.
- The doll: cr_sam@0:28-0:36 is the clearest, in daylight. Also cr_wasnt@1:00-1:03, cr_cz_released@4:24-4:36 (in the Bible-page closet), cr_trapped@5:36-5:48, and cr_wheel@7:48-8:00 (at the dining table).
- Orphans and Sister Charlotte arriving: MISSING. The nearest are cr_sam@0:38-0:42 (Charlotte and the girls unloading groceries from the truck, daytime) and cr_wheel@0:00-0:40 (Charlotte with Janice).
- Janice thrown off the stairlift: MISSING (see PROOF 1).
- Janice wheeled into the barn: see PROOF 2.
- Possession (black bile): see PROOF 3.
- Samuel's fingers and his death: fingers as in PROOF 4. The body is in cr_sam@0:59-1:01 (wide, mild) and cr_wheel@9:30-9:31 (close dead face) [GORE-light]. The killing blow is never shown.
- Esther alive, non-gory: cr_esther_scenes@0:58-1:12 and @1:36-2:02 (younger, unscarred), @2:34-2:44 (in Bee's room before the attack); scarred but not bloody at cr_esther_scenes@0:08-0:34 and @3:10-3:28, and cr_wasnt@0:38-0:58. In the dark: cr_esther_scenes@1:18-1:34. AVOID cr_esther_scenes@3:00-3:08 and cr_wasnt@0:30-0:37 (bloody eye, GORE).
- Linda at the well: see PROOF 5.
- The scarecrow: cr_scarecrow@0:26-0:28 (full figure on the wall), @0:32-0:42 and @0:50-0:52 (close-ups); also cr_cz_scarecrow@2:16-2:56, and outside by the barn at cr_cz_orphan@4:00-4:20 / cr_cz_scarecrow@0:24-0:42.
- Sister Charlotte locking Janice and the doll in the closet: cr_trapped@6:02-6:26 (she shoves them in and slams the doors at 6:12-6:22).
- Janice escaping through the hole in the closet wall: MISSING.
- The Higgins couple killed 12 years later: cr_end@0:00-0:47 ("12 YEARS LATER" caption 0:00-0:07; Pete clutching his cut throat with grown Janice/"Annabelle" beside him at 0:40-0:42 [GORE-light]; Sharon on the phone 0:24-0:46). Annabelle Higgins' death is not in any clip.

## The Conjuring: The Devil Made Me Do It (2021)

Notes for the whole film block:
- "Conjuring Archives" clips (dm_altar, dm_ar_*, dm_calvary, dm_heart, dm_promise, dm_truth, dm_vision) all carry the same burned-in logo (two faces + "THE CONJURING Archives") twice: top centre-left AND bottom centre-left, sitting in the black letterbox bars, on every frame for the whole clip. Crop to the picture area (2.39:1 inside 16:9) to lose it.
- ClipZone clips (co_stab, dm_cz_*, dm_david) have no in-picture bug; a blue "CLIPZONE" end card closes each one.
- Character IDs used below: Ed and Lorraine Warren; Arne Johnson (young man, wet dark hair, plaid shirt); Debbie Glatzel (Arne's girlfriend, long dark hair); David Glatzel (boy, big glasses); Bruno (the landlord: heavy-set, long curly hair, white tank top); Father Gordon (bald prison priest, rosary); Father Kastner (old man, John Noble, lamp-lit desk); Isla, the occultist (thin woman, dark hair pulled back, black high-collared dress); Drew (Ed's assistant, red shirt); Sgt Clay (Black police officer, tie).
- Not seen in any clip: Jessica's cliff fall; the stabbing of Katie; Arne visibly stabbing Bruno in light.

### Proof moments (DMMDI)
1. Waterbed bulging, hand grabbing up through it: dm_ar_waterbed@1:38-1:51 (clearest, includes the burst and David dragged in); alt dm_cz_nightmare@2:28-2:47.
2. 1981 exorcism, David contorting, Ed clutching chest and going down: contorting dm_david@2:50-3:13 (David arched on the table, 3:10); Ed's heart attack dm_heart@0:44-1:03 and 1:22-1:35, or dm_david@5:22-5:25 and 5:36-5:43.
3. Arne walking down the road covered in blood: dm_bruno_it@3:16-3:47 or dm_cz_archie@4:58-5:29 show the aftermath walk (dazed, wet, sunken eyes, police car arrives), but NO blood is readable on him in either clip. Bloody version MISSING.
4. Morgue corpse rising and charging: co_stab@3:42-3:53 (rise 3:42, charge 3:46-3:51); dm_ar_letgo@1:10-1:23.
5. Ed swinging the sledgehammer at Lorraine in the tunnel: dm_final@0:44-0:51 (swing at 0:50); dm_ar_love@0:00-0:06 and 0:46-0:52; dm_cz_herself@4:44-4:49 and 5:30-5:35.
6. Ward windows exploding, Arne floating above the bed (stop before the shard): dm_ar_love@0:08-0:24 (floating wide 0:20-0:21; shard appears 0:28, cut before it); dm_cz_herself@4:38-4:43; dm_final@0:54-0:57. Windows bursting clearly: unclear (glass and debris visible, no clean window-burst shot found).
7. Isla's body twisted by her demon (wide): dm_promise@0:40-0:41 (wide, Isla doubled over) and 0:58-1:04 (wide, collapse and body); tighter beats 0:42-0:57 (0:56-0:57 demon face, GORE-medium).
8. Never show Jessica's cliff fall or the stabbing of Katie: neither appears in any clip. Also avoid the self-harm wrist shots: co_stab@1:26-1:58, dm_calvary@0:24-0:33 and 1:00-1:01, plus the shard shots dm_ar_love@0:28-0:45, dm_final@0:26-0:27 and 1:12-1:19, dm_cz_herself@5:12-5:29.

Key questions:
- Cursed charm/totem hidden in the home, found under the house: dm_cz_nightmare@6:32-7:19 (totem clear 6:58-6:59 and 7:04-7:05; Lorraine photographs it 7:10-7:19); the crawlspace entry 4:14-4:55. Possible planting of an object under a table: dm_bruno_b@0:00-0:01, dm_bruno_it@0:10-0:11, dm_cz_archie@1:52-1:55 (unclear what it is).
- Waterbed: see proof 1.
- David's 1981 exorcism and Ed's heart attack: see proof 2 (dm_david whole clip; dm_heart).
- Arne and Bruno before: dm_cz_archie@0:56-1:09 (Bruno friendly at the truck in daylight); party dm_bruno_b@0:02-1:35 / dm_bruno_it@0:12-1:47. After: the kennel killing is in the dark and barely visible (dm_bruno_it@2:56-3:09, dm_bruno_b@2:42-2:55); then the road walk (proof 3).
- Arne walking bloody on the road: walk present, blood not visible (see proof 3).
- Jessica and Katie alive together: dm_vision@1:42-2:07 (two girls smiling, bracelet passed). Possessed girl: dm_vision@2:36-2:39. Which girl is Katie and which is Jessica is presumed, not confirmed by name on screen.
- Katie's body in the morgue: covered body on the slab co_stab@0:36-0:39; Lorraine's hand on the corpse co_stab@3:36-3:37 / dm_ar_letgo@1:04-1:05. That this body is Katie is not confirmed on screen (the corpse that rises looks bulky with long curly hair).
- Lorraine's vision: in the morgue, of Isla and Arne co_stab@0:16-2:27 / dm_calvary; in the woods, of the girls dm_vision@0:20-2:53.
- Morgue corpse charging: proof 4.
- Kastner: dm_truth@0:06-0:43 and 1:58-2:53 (talking, with Isla's photos); dm_altar@0:17-0:19 and 0:32-0:51.
- Kastner's death: dm_altar@0:52-1:03 (Isla pulls his head back at the desk) and the flash 1:17-1:21 (bloody blade); the killing itself is not clearly shown.
- The occultist Isla: clearest face co_stab@1:44-1:47; dm_promise@0:14-0:23; attacking Lorraine dm_cz_herself@0:31-0:45 / dm_isla@0:30-0:45; hands over Ed's eyes dm_cz_herself@1:42-1:47.
- Ed attacking Lorraine with the sledgehammer in the tunnel: proof 5.
- Isla twisted by her demon: proof 7.
- Altar smashed by Ed: dm_ar_love@1:44-1:57 (best, clean wides 1:49-1:53); dm_final@2:28-2:41. Lorraine finding the altar before: dm_altar@1:59-2:58.

## The Conjuring: Last Rites (2025)

Notes for all clips: the timecodes are clip seconds (m:ss). They were checked against full-size ffmpeg grabs. The contact sheets run up to about 1 s off in fast cuts. Character names used: Ed, Lorraine, Judy (adult, bangs), Tony (Judy's boyfriend), Jack and Janet Smurl (parents; Jack has a moustache), Heather (eldest Smurl daughter, dark curly hair), Dawn (Smurl daughter, blue headband), Shannon (little Smurl girl with braids), Father Gordon. Ghosts: the "axe man" (long hair, denim overalls, grin, lantern), the "old woman"/hag (white hair, black-ringed eyes, rotten grin). I never saw a separate third "grinning man" ghost identified on screen (see the summary).

### Proof moments (Last Rites)
1. 1964 delivery room. Mirror entity: lr_s01_baby@1:46-1:50 (entity in surgeon's cap at the window, dark) and @2:21-2:23 (lightning flash: barefoot entity in a gown crouched over Lorraine; best). Doctors over the silent baby: lr_birth@0:40-0:47 (nurse with the limp newborn by flashlight; also lr_s01_baby@4:12-4:31). Baby born dead, then Lorraine praying her back: lr_birth@0:54-1:40 (dead baby on her chest, prayer), revival @1:44-2:17. Mirror reflection smiling at Lorraine: lr_s01_baby@0:18-0:21. AVOID the hanging legs at lr_s01_baby@0:21-0:23.
2. Kitchen light smashing on the table: lr_preview@4:07-4:08 (fixture shatters from the ceiling, low angle), aftermath @4:08-4:27. It is a dinner table; the birthday cake and candle are @3:36-3:59 at the same table, just before.
3. Dawn coughing blood and glass (reaction/wide): lr_s03_blood@2:50-2:51 (wide: Jack grabs Dawn), @2:54-2:55 (family reacting), @3:14-3:21 (Heather's horrified face, clean). Avoid @2:44-2:47, @2:52-2:53, @2:56-3:01, @3:08-3:13 (gore close-ups).
4. Jack floating, Janet pulling him down: lr_family@1:26-1:27 (best wide, Janet asleep below), @1:18-1:41 (float); pulled down at lr_s05_axe@0:28-0:31 (also lr_family@5:00-5:05, lr_axe@4:08-4:15).
5. Mirror whole in the attic: lr_cz_comp@3:50-3:59 (whole carved mirror lit by Judy's flashlight). Also lr_rt_keepmoving@0:56-0:59 (wrapped mirror being moved in the attic; three faces).
6. Mirror pinning Tony: lr_end@2:26-2:29 (brief, face under the mirror with flying glass) and @3:40-3:43 (wounded on the floor). The Bible burning in Ed's hands: lr_end@1:22-1:25.
7. Never use: Father Gordon's fall at lr_priest@2:22-2:37 (also the cord @0:16-0:25); Judy's rope at lr_exorcism@2:40-4:11; the 1964 hanging at lr_s01_baby@0:21-0:23 (and the crooked-neck old man at lr_open@0:46-0:47).

Key questions:
- 1964 birth (entity, silent baby, prayer): see 1 above.
- Old mirror: lr_s01_baby@0:00-0:10 and @0:26-0:27 (1964 shop); lr_preview@2:19-2:21 (unwrapped as a gift) and @3:00-3:11 (on the kitchen wall); lr_s03_blood@1:42-1:50 (thrown in the garbage truck); lr_cz_comp@3:50-3:59 (attic); lr_end@0:28-0:45 (glowing on the landing).
- Axe man: lr_s10_axeghost@0:42-0:45 and @0:58-1:07 (lantern, grin, best); lr_axe@3:54-3:55 (close face); lr_s05_axe@0:10-0:15 (dark).
- Old woman: lr_cz_comp@1:32-1:33 (under the wedding dress, bright, best); lr_s02_suzie@2:34-2:35 (with the doll); lr_mirror@1:50-1:51; lr_s10_axeghost@0:24-0:27 (shushing).
- Grinning man (third ghost): MISSING. No separate grinning-man ghost was identifiable. The axe man grins in every shot. lr_end@3:20-3:21 shows a grinning man, identity unclear.
- Smurl family: lr_preview@0:00-0:31, @1:26-1:55, @2:32-2:59 (kitchen table, wide); lr_s05_axe@0:40-0:59 (on the stairs); lr_s03_blood@1:00-1:25 (breakfast).
- Dawn blood and glass: see 3.
- Kitchen light on the table: see 2.
- Jack floating: see 4.
- Father Gordon (safe shots only): lr_priest@0:00-0:15, @0:26-1:28 (office, phone), @2:02-2:21 (match flame, eyes). Nothing after 2:21.
- Ed's heart attack: lr_s09_possessed@2:24-2:29 (Ed clutches his chest) and lr_ar_eviljudy@1:36-1:41 (clearer); both dark.
- Judy possessed or attacked: lr_s09_possessed@1:40-1:41 (white eyes, bloody mouth; best), @0:40-0:43 (crawling); lr_end@1:48-1:49 (bloody grin); attacked: lr_mirror@2:40-2:47 (choking in the bridal mirrors) and lr_s08_giant@1:38-2:31 (giant Annabelle chase, fall).
- Lorraine praying Judy back (1986): lr_exorcism@4:18-5:11 (prayer), @5:18-5:23 (Judy opens her eyes). The rubble just before it (3:42-4:11) is in the rope aftermath, so avoid it.
- Bible burning: lr_end@1:22-1:25.
- Ed's exorcism failing: lr_end@0:24-0:27 (reading, hand raised), @0:38-0:45 (the mirror flares), @0:52-1:03 (Ed thrown and hurt), @1:22-1:25 (Bible burns).
- Family breaking the mirror together: lr_end@2:46-2:55 (three pairs of hands on the mirror), @2:56-3:11 (Judy screams, the mirror explodes in light), @3:44-3:45 (carved faces broken).
- Annabelle in Last Rites: lr_s04_watching@0:28-0:31 and @0:42-0:47 (in the rocking chair, dark); lr_preview@5:15-5:19 (photo on the lecture screen); lr_s08_giant@1:38-1:50 (giant Annabelle silhouette); lr_cz_comp@5:56-6:11 (giant Annabelle sitting, then seen from behind in her white dress with red sash).

## The Nun II (2023)

Notes on method: every sheet was read, then the important moments were checked with 1-4 fps strips and full-size grabs (in SP\zoom\n2\). Times are clip seconds as m:ss. Shots that cut fast are accurate to about +-1 s. Where clips are re-uploads of the same footage, the offset is given.

Character key, as seen: Irene (nun, dark hair, white collar band), Debra (young Black novice in white headscarf), Sophie (girl with long braids, striped red/grey sweater with overalls), Kate (teacher, dark hair, white blouse), Maurice/"Frenchie" (curly hair, braces), Madame Laurent (headmistress, white hair, black dress), Jacques (altar boy, dark hair), Father Noiret (bald older priest), Valak (white-faced nun, yellow eyes).

### Proof moments (The Nun II)
1. Noiret lifted above the altar and burned, Jacques watching: n2_burn 6:10.5-6:14.5 (rise 6:10.5-6:11.5, flames 6:12-6:13, Jacques reacts 6:13-6:14.5 and screams 6:16-6:17.5). Cut before the 6:15 burning close-up. Alternate: co_burn_4k 2:33-2:37.
2. The delivery kid lifted by the throat: n2_deliv. The kid runs out 2:10-2:14; the grab against the wall starts at about 2:17.2 (on the choke close-up 2:17.5-2:18.2); feet dangle 2:18.5-2:19.7. The second before any snap: 2:19.7. No snap or blood is shown on screen; the clip hard-cuts at 2:20.0. Lift begins 2:17.2-2:17.5. Kid's sex: ambiguous. The cap and work shirt read as a boy at a glance, but close-ups (0:36-0:48) look like a girl with curly hair tucked under the cap. Do not voice the sex. Alternate: n2_dr_deliv 2:05.5-2:06.2 (cropped picture, ends at the grab). co_neck and co_neck_b do NOT contain this kid.
3. The magazine stand flipping into Valak, Valak lunges at Irene: co_neck 1:12-2:13 (face forms 1:12-1:17, full grid 1:58-2:12, real Valak lunges 2:13). Alternate: co_neck_b 1:42-2:43.
4. Madame Laurent follows "Cedric" into the chapel; cut before the blows: n2_altar 1:12-1:34 (boy in her flashlight 1:12-1:16, her hand on his shoulder 1:24-1:27, face turns 1:33-1:34). First blow is at 1:35; cut by 1:34.5. Alternate: co_neck_b 9:16-9:39.
5. The goat demon charging the girls: MISSING. No goat is clearly visible in n2_cz_goat, n2_goat, or n2_lucy. The nearest is the unseen-force dorm attack in n2_goat 1:21-1:31 (n2_cz_goat 4:47-4:56), and the goat in the stained glass in n2_ar_defy 0:46-0:56 and 1:52-1:56.
6. Possessed Maurice / body bending and snapping: no contortion or bone-snapping is shown in any clip (MISSING for the bending). Possessed Maurice: n2_cz_maurice 3:56 (head back) and 4:04-4:09 (grin); n2_mc_possess 2:00-2:07; bleeding eyes and collapse: n2_cz_returns 1:04-1:15; possessed again: n2_cz_returns 2:24-2:30.
7. Irene lifted and set alight, and the fire going out: n2_lucy 0:12-0:57.4 (lift 0:12, flames 0:23, burning 0:31-0:47, unhurt 0:50, fire out 0:56.8-0:57.4). Alternate for the lift and fire only: n2_cz_returns 4:54-4:58 and 5:41-5:55.
- Saint Lucy relic (her eyes): n2_cz_returns 0:33 (handed over), 0:41, 0:50-0:51 (round relic case held up, glowing rim); n2_maurice 0:08 and 0:28. The eyes themselves are never shown inside the case. Also the St. Lucy painting in n2_burn 2:16-2:22.
- Wine turning to blood in the barrels: n2_exorcise 1:26-1:36 (barrels burst into red spray over Valak and Maurice); n2_lucy 2:08-2:14. Valak bursts into fire: n2_exorcise 1:42-1:44.
- Irene's mother / family flashback: n2_exorcise 1:15-1:17 (little Irene in her mother's arms, bright white). The vision in co_neck 2:16-2:45 shows a nun silhouette, a pyre, and a red-robed figure over a kneeling woman; its identity is unclear (reads as a saint's martyrdom).
- Deaths across Europe: MISSING (not shown in any clip).
- Valak at the newsstand: see Proof 3.

## The Nun (2018)

Characters: Sister Irene (Taissa Farmiga; white novice habit, or white nightgown with long loose hair), Father Burke (Demián Bichir; black priest coat, collar), Frenchie (Jonas Bloquet; curly hair, white shirt, braces, red neckerchief, shotgun), Valak (black habit, white wimple, grey face, yellow eyes, fangs), Sister Victoria (young nun of the opening; any shot of her at the window, with the rope, or hanging is SUICIDE), the older nun of the opening (bloodied, dragged into the dark), Daniel (ghost boy, snake in his mouth).
Clips that repeat the same footage: n1_ar_defeats = n1_cz_fight 2:54-5:50 = co_valak0 0:00-2:10 = spooky_nun 7:02-8:46 (flooded crypt). co_valak0 = n1_cz_possessed shifted by 5:06 (co 0:00 = cz 5:06). n1_wb_possessed ~ n1_cz_fight 0:00-2:52 ~ n1_cz_possessed 1:28-4:20. co_buried_b ~ n1_ar_buried ~ n1_cz_buried 4:48-8:02 ~ spooky_nun 2:14-4:40. n1_open ~ n1_wb_hanging ~ spooky_nun 8:48-12:20.

### Proof moments (The Nun)
- PROOF 1, corridor of crosses + "God ends here" door + nun yanked into the dark:
  - Corridor of crosses: n1_ar_darkness@3:12-3:26 (Irene with a candle, crosses on posts, dark) or spooky_nun@20:16-20:22 (Irene running between the crosses) / @18:22-18:30. The crosses stand or lean on posts in these shots. A corridor of HANGING crosses is unclear, not seen.
  - "Finit hic Deo" inscription: n1_ar_darkness@3:28 (one clear beat, about 2 s) or spooky_nun@18:38.
  - Nun yanked into the dark: n1_wb_hanging@0:22-0:25 (the bloodied older nun lurches and is pulled back into the black doorway), plus the bloody hand and rosary @0:10. Alternatives: n1_open@0:36-0:56 (a hand reaching out of the dark doorway, the bloody hand @0:40, the older nun's bloody face @0:42-0:52) or spooky_nun@8:54-9:12. The pull itself is very dark, so brighten it.
- PROOF 2, Burke buried alive with the bell, Irene digging:
  - Bell and string: co_buried_b@0:20-0:22 (bell on its post); Burke pulling the string inside the coffin @1:38-1:48.
  - Burke in the coffin with the lighter: co_buried_b@1:14-1:34; screaming @0:32.
  - Irene digging: co_buried_b@0:52 (by hand), @2:06-2:10 (shovel), @2:24 (kneeling at the grave).
  - Burke's gravestone [TEXT]: n1_cz_buried@1:22-1:26 or n1_cz_graveyard@2:48-2:50. Lid closing: n1_cz_buried@1:14-1:18.
  - Burke climbs out: n1_ar_buried@2:46-2:50.
- PROOF 3, the abbess, then the nuns revealed as never alive:
  - Abbess: n1_hl_abbess@1:00-1:20, @1:42-1:52, @2:02-2:06 (has a burned-in subtitle), @2:40-2:42, @2:56-3:06. She is a veiled black silhouette behind a carved screen; her face is never shown.
  - Prayer and then the empty habits: n1_pray@0:20-0:34 (Irene praying among the nuns) followed by @0:40-0:58 (overhead, only empty habits on the floor). The same beats are at n1_cz_takeover@2:14-2:34 and @2:40-2:58. Brighten.
  - Dead nuns, extra: n1_cz_possessed@1:00-1:18 (rows of faceless black-habit nuns facing Burke); hooded dead nuns n1_cz_fight@0:06-0:54.
- PROOF 4, Victoria's corpse rising at Frenchie: n1_cz_takeover@8:54 (corpse nun lunges up screaming at a man who looks like Frenchie; a quick cut, GORE-light). Lead-in: uncovering the rotted face @8:04-8:08. Whether the man is Frenchie and whether the corpse is Victoria cannot be confirmed by eye, but it is likely.
- PROOF 5, Valak's face under the water as Irene drowns: n1_ar_defeats@1:16-1:21 (Irene pulled under, then Valak's blurred face through the water at 1:19-1:21); Irene drowning, eyes closed @1:52-1:58. Alternatives: n1_cz_fight@4:46-4:52 (brighter grade); co_valak0@0:40 and @0:56; spooky_nun@7:52 and @8:08. Valak rising out of the water: n1_ar_defeats@0:42-0:54.
- PROOF 6, Frenchie's CPR, then the inverted cross on his neck:
  - CPR: n1_cz_possessed@7:24-8:02 (lifts her out 7:24, CPR 7:42, mouth-to-mouth 7:48-7:50, lifeless 7:52-7:58, revives 8:00-8:02). The same beats are at co_valak0@2:18-2:56.
  - Inverted cross on Frenchie's neck: MISSING (in none of the clips; it would need a clip of the ending, the "six months later" glow-on-the-neck beat or the Annabelle Comes Home/Conjuring tie-in).
- PROOF 7, never show Victoria at the window or hanging (SUICIDE ranges to exclude): n1_open@1:16-1:18 (rope), @2:40-3:08 (window), @3:50-3:58 (hanging, crow-eaten face); n1_wb_hanging@0:46, @2:10-2:38, @3:20-3:30; spooky_nun@9:34, @10:58-11:26, @12:08-12:16. Also take care with the abbey wall and crow shots just before the hanging (n1_open@3:38-3:44, n1_wb_hanging@3:10-3:14, spooky_nun@11:56-12:02), because the body hangs at that same door. No hanging body is visible in the frames I checked, but check before use.
- Key question, the abbey in Romania (exteriors): n1_hl_abbess@3:36-3:38 (dusk, clearest); n1_ar_darkness@2:24-2:26 (night, brighten); n1_cz_buried@2:34 and @2:52-2:54 (courtyard at night, dark).
- Key question, the doorway to Hell ("Finit hic Deo"): n1_ar_darkness@3:28; spooky_nun@18:38. An actual opening to Hell in the crypt floor is unclear: the crypt-floor hole sealing at n1_cz_possessed@7:44-7:46 and the floor hatch at n1_ar_darkness@2:54-3:02 cannot be identified by eye.
- Key question, the opening nun dragged into the dark: see PROOF 1, n1_wb_hanging@0:22-0:25.
- Key question, the dead nuns (empty habits, empty chapel after the prayer): see PROOF 3, n1_pray@0:40-0:58.
- Key question, the abbess: see PROOF 3, n1_hl_abbess@1:00-1:20.
- Key question, Burke buried alive with the bell, and Irene digging: see PROOF 2, co_buried_b@1:38-1:48 and @2:06-2:10.
- Key question, Irene drowned with Valak's face under the water: see PROOF 5, n1_ar_defeats@1:16-1:21.
- Key question, Frenchie's CPR: see PROOF 6, n1_cz_possessed@7:42-8:02.
- Key question, the vial of the blood of Christ: n1_ar_defeats@2:08 (gold-caged vial, close-up); n1_cz_fight@4:44 (vial in Irene's hand) and @5:02; n1_cz_possessed@6:08-6:12 and @6:30-6:32; co_valak0@1:02-1:06 and @1:24. Irene spitting the blood: n1_ar_defeats@2:14, n1_cz_fight@5:12.
- Key question, Valak defeated: n1_ar_defeats@2:20-2:50 (bloody face, clawing at her burning face GORE, sinking @2:50); n1_cz_fight@5:14-5:48 (brighter; sinking @5:28-5:32).
- Key question, Valak escaping into Frenchie (inverted cross on his neck): MISSING.
- Key question, Valak face close-ups: co_valak0@0:30 (screaming, very large); n1_cz_fight@4:08, @4:34, @4:42; n1_ar_defeats@1:10, @1:14, @1:48; spooky_nun@18:12 and @0:18 (yellow eyes, extreme close-up); spooky_nun@12:34-12:48 (full figure behind Irene, well lit); n1_wb_possessed@2:50. Do not use n1_cz_fight@1:58-2:04 or n1_wb_possessed@2:00-2:06 as Valak: that face is the possessed Irene in a white habit.
